# Employee Canonical Mapping Proposal

Implementation-ready mapping specification. Canonical Python classes and mapper code are not implemented by this document. The implementation contract below governs all inventory entries.


Start with the **Implementation contract** at the end; it governs the inventory and accepted code tables.

## Read this first

- The visible label Employee ID proposes the Python field employee_id, matching the current naming direction. It means the selected source employee identifier (previously called source_employee_id), not a newly generated global ID.
- Populate provider from adapter identity: prismhr, isolved, or ukg-ready. Preserve available client/legal/EIN scope; provider plus employee_id is not asserted to be globally unique across every tenant.
- Employee Number remains separate. UKG employee_id is preserved as ukg_employee_id, not used as the retrieval identifier.
- Preserve nulls where accepted; do not fabricate missing fields, defaults, enum meanings, units, or IDs.
- Provider-prefixed dotted targets live under provider_fields.<provider>; explicitly named flat targets remain fields on the canonical employee. source_metadata and source_extras are separate containers.
- Source array markers [] retain record boundaries. Repeated endpoint views are not separate canonical fields to merge automatically; apply the explicit conflict and collection rules in the implementation contract.
- Descriptions and verified source references are in [Employee Data Expanded](Employee%20Data%20Expanded.md). This proposal uses that inventory's current source types.
- Existing requiredness policy applies to source validation. Use the output shape in the implementation contract; source requiredness is unchanged.

## Mapping actions

| Action | Meaning |
|---|---|
| Direct | Copy/rename an accepted shared meaning; retain the value. |
| Convert | Apply the deterministic helper rules in the implementation contract; no helpers are implemented by this document. |
| Preserve separately | Keep the source information without claiming equivalence to another provider's field. |
| Preserve without translation | A missing dictionary is an evidence gap: retain the source value and record an issue instead of guessing a shared label. |

## Current decisions and implementation notes

You do not need to approve the complete field inventory individually. These rules apply to whole groups. Types and destinations below follow the implementation contract; no mapper or canonical model has changed.

| Topic | Decision made | What remains |
|---|---|---|
| IDs | Canonical identifiers use strings. Numeric isolved and UKG IDs need conversion; existing string IDs stay unchanged. | Implement one ID helper, including whole-number float formatting and existing null acceptance. |
| Pronouns | Use one preferred_pronouns value. Identical input values appear once, not as duplicate JSON keys. | If the two isolved values differ, preserve both source values without silently overwriting; conflict handling still needs implementation. |
| Gender | Separate ordinary gender, isolved EEO override, and PrismHR designation. | Use the approved complete labels. Prism M/F/X/D meanings are user assumptions; U is a user extension, B remains unmapped. |
| Ethnicity | Keep ethnic code, ethnic-origin description, and Hispanic boolean separate until mappings are verified. | No shared category/code list is required now; preserve source-specific values. No employee-ethnicity counterpart is identified in the current UKG models. |
| Dates | Use Python date for canonical dates, serialized as YYYY-MM-DD. Conversion belongs in a shared helper. | Implement blank/invalid/timezone handling there. Preserve original timestamps when needed rather than silently losing information. |
| Compensation | Use numeric values, keeping annual/per-pay/hourly amounts and duration distinct. Do not assume unit or currency conversions. | Use finite float values for this implementation; do not round, rescale or convert currency. Decimal handling is a future improvement. |
| Separate fields | Every Preserve separately entry stays separate and is not a fallback for a shared field. | Proposed types are now provided; Use provider_fields for dotted provider-owned targets; see the output contract. |
| Extras | Keep unknown input fields outside shared business fields. | Pydantic preserves undeclared fields but does not type-check their contents. Mapping must explicitly preserve nested extras too. |

### Employment status and type: the exact fields

| Meaning | PrismHR | isolved | UKG Ready | What we need |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Employment status | client.employeeStatus | employmentStatus | status | Their actual lookup meanings before translating into one shared vocabulary. |
| Employment status class | client.statusClass | No confirmed equivalent | No confirmed equivalent | Keep separate. ClientMaster.statusClass in the lookup file is company status, not this employee field. |
| Employee type | client.employeeType | employmentCategoryCode | pay_info.employee_type.id | Each provider's dictionary; a string code and a lookup ID are not interchangeable. |
| Employee type class | client.typeClass | No confirmed equivalent | No confirmed equivalent | Keep separate from employee type. |

These can remain source-specific until their meanings are available. See [Employee Code Mapping Review](Employee%20Code%20Mapping%20Review.md) for available codes and missing dictionaries.

### Compensation: a concrete proposal

| Shared field | Proposed Python type | Source / meaning |
|---|---|---|
| annual_salary | Optional[float] | isolved annualSalary. PrismHR annualPay stays separate while its description is missing. |
| per_pay_salary | Optional[float] | isolved perPaySalary; PrismHR salary with its documented payMethod=S/pay-period context. |
| hourly_rate | Optional[float] | isolved hourlyRate and PrismHR hourlyPay. |
| other_pay_rate + other_pay_rate_basis | Optional[float] + Optional[str] | PrismHR hourlyPayRate plus hourlyPayPeriod; the basis may be hourly, weekly, monthly, etc. |
| standard_hours_per_pay_period | Optional[float] | PrismHR standardHours and isolved normalHours. |
| Historical compensation amounts | Optional[float] within separate history records | Keep UKG currency/amount_period and isolved salary-history context with each record. |
| ukg_standard_work_day_milliseconds | Optional[int] | Preserve the documented milliseconds separately. |

Parsing a numeric string does not change its basis: a monthly amount does not become annual salary. Currency remains unknown unless supplied. Use finite float values in the first mapper; no rounding or financial calculations are introduced.

### Other related fields: concrete examples

| Fields | Difference | Keep them this way for now |
|---|---|---|
| PrismHR compensation.formI9Filed; isolved i9Documents.i9Completed | On file versus completed. | Separate flags unless equivalence is established. |
| PrismHR person.hrpCitizenshipCountry / compensation.citizenshipStatus; isolved citizenship | Country versus status code versus description. | Separate fields. |
| PrismHR person.handicapped / client.selfIdDisabilityForm; isolved disabled | A flag versus a response that can include DeclineToAnswer. | Preserve the response; do not turn declining to answer into false. |
| PrismHR client.unionCode; isolved jobIsUnion; UKG pay_info.union.id | Code/ID versus membership boolean. | Separate code, ID, and boolean. |
| PrismHR client.jobCode; isolved jobCode/jobId; UKG pay_info.default_job.id | Business code versus record/lookup ID. | Preserve both code and identifier. |
| PrismHR employer IDs; isolved legalCode; UKG ein.id | Different company/legal reference schemes. | Preserve source scope; do not claim they identify the same company. |
| State name/code, ZIP/suffix, national-ID type/value | Related values have different roles. | Keep their components; combine only with a documented rule. |

### Extras: what validation actually does

Recommended: source_extras: dict[str, Any] on each canonical record, retaining original nested paths. Known provider-specific fields stay named fields; undeclared extras stay in source_extras. Neither should overwrite shared fields.

Pydantic accepts/preserves unknown extras; it does not validate their contents against a declared field type. A mapper must extract model_extra recursively for nested models. They are not returned automatically.

Alternative: retain the complete original source record beside the canonical record. That helps traceability but duplicates already-mapped information.

## Specific proposals already discussed

- Preferred name: PrismHR preferredName + isolved nameAddress.preferredName.
- Nickname: PrismHR person.nickname + UKG nickname; no automatic preferred-name fallback.
- Salutation/prefix and suffix: preserve the user-approved correspondences.
- isolved uniqueId -> isolved_derived_employee_unique_id; UKG external_id -> ukg_external_id.
- UKG primary_account_id -> ukg_primary_account_id; optional and documented only for multi-EIN companies.
- UKG username -> ukg_username; PrismHR person.userId -> prismhr_portal_user_id.
- PrismHR person.hispanic -> is_hispanic, independent of ethnicity code/description.
- isolved eeoGenderOverride -> isolved_eeo_gender_override; this is a reporting override, not ordinary gender.
- Canonical dates use a shared helper and serialize as YYYY-MM-DD; blank, invalid, and timezone cases belong in that helper.
- For marital status, approved labels are single, married, divorced, widowed, civil union and other. Unrecognized values are preserved, not assigned an invented meaning.

## Documented lookup boundaries

- PrismHR supplied lookup-reference.json has no ethnic-code entries. OpenAPI GET /clientMaster/v1/getClientCodes documents options=Ethnic for client-specific codes.
- GET /codeFiles/v1/getEeoCodes with eeoCodeType=Ethnic concerns EEO setup codes. Do not assume that dictionary substitutes for employee client ethnic codes.
- isolved ethnicOrigin is documented only as an Ethnic Origin description; categories and equivalence are not established.
- PrismHR maritalStatus meanings are documented. isolved's description says only Marital status code; no cross-provider enum translation is assumed.
- UKG standard_work_day is milliseconds. It is not directly equivalent to standard/normal hours per pay period.
- PrismHR firstName's description conflicts with the field name. Its shared-name correspondence is based on the user's accepted review and remains a documentation gap.

## Complete per-field mapping inventory

Reference only: these are not decisions you must approve individually. Provider prefixes indicate separate ownership rather than a mandatory final JSON layout. Preserve separately entries remain separate. Unknown extras are outside this inventory and are not coerced by guessing their names.

| Provider | Source field (Python type) | Proposed canonical destination | Proposed canonical Python type | Action | Reason |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| prismhr | `id` (str) | `employee_id` | `Optional[str]` | Convert | Selected source employee ID is a string. Convert numeric isolved/UKG IDs; preserve existing strings and null acceptance. |
| prismhr | `lastName` (Optional[str]) | `last_name` | `Optional[str]` | Direct | Copy the supplied surname. |
| prismhr | `firstName` (Optional[str]) | `first_name` | `Optional[str]` | Direct | User-approved correspondence; copy as supplied. PrismHR description conflicts with its field name and remains flagged. |
| prismhr | `middleInitial` (Optional[str]) | `middle_name` | `Optional[str]` | Direct | Preserve the supplied initial/name; do not expand an initial into a full name. |
| prismhr | `preferredName` (Optional[str]) | `preferred_name` | `Optional[str]` | Direct | Keep preferred name separate from nickname, as requested. |
| prismhr | `person` (Optional[PrismHRPerson]) | `prismhr.person` | `Optional[dict[str, Any]]` | Preserve separately | Preserve the source container and its independently listed children; this is not automatic merging or copying into a shared field. |
| prismhr | `person.ethnicCode` (Optional[str]) | `prismhr_ethnic_code` | `Optional[str]` | Preserve separately | Keep the client code until its Ethnic lookup and a shared-category crosswalk are available. |
| prismhr | `person.birthDate` (Optional[str]) | `birth_date` | `Optional[date]` | Convert | Use one date helper targeting YYYY-MM-DD. Review malformed values and datetime/timezone handling before implementing. Use the shared date helper for YYYY-MM-DD; blank, invalid, and timezone handling belongs there. |
| prismhr | `person.driverLicenseId` (Optional[str]) | `prismhr.person.driver_license_id` | `Optional[str]` | Preserve separately | No established shared counterpart; retain the source value/type and nested context. Canonical IDs use strings; numeric IDs require the ID helper. |

## Verification

- prismhr: 392 source paths with proposed canonical types.
- isolved: 578 source paths with proposed canonical types.
- ukg_ready: 200 source paths with proposed canonical types.
- Convert: 36 entries.
- Direct: 46 entries.
- Preserve separately: 1058 entries.
- Blocking business decisions: 0. Missing source dictionaries use the documented preservation fallback.

No source models, canonical models, or adapters changed. Helpers and canonical types still need implementation.


## Verified code evidence and accepted decisions

**Decided** means the shape/preservation decision is accepted; missing dictionaries use the implementation fallback. No mapper or source model changes are included.

### Decisions

| Topic | Status | Current decision |
|---|---|---|
| Gender | Decided | Keep ordinary gender, EEO override and Prism designation distinct. Preserve original provider values. |
| Status / status class | Decided | Separate fields; matching one sample value does not make them equivalent. |
| Type / type class | Decided | Separate fields; Prism sample employeeType=FT, typeClass=F. |
| Rehire | Decided | eligible / ineligible / no preference; missing/null remains unavailable. |
| State | Decided | state_code is separate from provider-specific state text; do not infer name/code equivalence. |
| Postal code | Decided | Combine ZIP and nonblank suffix with a hyphen; preserve original components. |
| Country | Decided | Preserve UKG country separately; remove it from shared matches. |
| IDs | Decided | Strings; preserve employer lists, legal references and EIN references independently. |
| Work location | Decided | Use work_location_code with provider scope; preserve display description and organization list separately. |
| Extras | Decided | Preserve known provider-specific fields and unknown extras separately at their original nesting. |

### Gender

| Source | Code/value | Meaning / proposed label | Evidence |
|---|---|---|---|
| Prism person.gender | M | male | User-selected interpretation; contract lists code only. |
| Prism person.gender | F | female | User-selected interpretation; contract lists code only. |
| Prism person.gender | X | non-binary | User-selected interpretation; contract lists code only. |
| Prism person.gender | D | declined | User-selected interpretation; contract lists code only. |
| Prism person.gender | U | unknown | User-selected extension; absent from this contract's code list. |
| Prism person.gender | B | Unresolved | Mentioned in request, but contract lists D, not B. Do not equate B with D. |
| isolved EmployeeModel.gender | Male | male | Observed Postman value; Swagger says gender description. |
| isolved other values | Not supplied | Preserve raw | No complete dictionary for this EmployeeModel field. |
| UKG | No identified counterpart | Preserve any separate source fields | Current comparison has no ordinary gender match. |

Swagger also contains a **different** Corestream.EmployeeModel with M=Male, F=Female, X=Nonbinary, N=ChoseNotToAnswer. That dictionary does not automatically apply to the EmployeeModel used here. No U/B meanings are inferred from it.

### Marital status

| Prism code | Documented label | Proposed shared label | isolved / UKG evidence |
|---|---|---|---|
| S | Single | single | isolved full-record sample supplies Married; no full dictionary. |
| M | Married | married | isolved Married can match this label by meaning. |
| D | Divorced | divorced | No verified isolved code mapping. |
| W | Widowed | widowed | No verified isolved code mapping. |
| C | Civil Union | civil union | No verified isolved code mapping. |
| O | Other | other | No verified isolved code mapping. |

UKG has no identified marital-status counterpart in the current models. isolved Swagger describes a one-character code, but the saved full-record response contains Married. Preserve that contract/sample discrepancy; do not truncate it to M automatically. Use the approved readable labels; unknown/unverified source codes remain preserved without translation.

### Employment status

| Provider/field | Codes or values verified locally | Meaning / handling |
|---|---|---|
| Prism client.employeeStatus | A observed | Client/provider-defined; A's meaning needs its own dictionary. |
| Prism client.statusClass | A / L / T | active / on_leave / terminated, explicitly documented. |
| Prism ClientMaster.employeeStatus[] | statusCodeId | Catalog schema supplies identifiers only; saved catalog is empty. No labels available. |
| isolved employmentStatus | Terminated observed | Description field; candidate normalized label terminated. Complete set not supplied. |
| UKG employee.status | No field-specific dictionary supplied | Preserve raw. Payroll status enums do not apply. |

**Keep status and class separate.** Class is a broad classification; employeeStatus is a configured status. The A/A sample cannot establish a general mapping. ClientMaster.statusClass is company status and must not be substituted for Client.statusClass.

### Employment type and catalog codes

| Provider/field | Available evidence | Handling |
|---|---|---|
| Prism client.employeeType | FT observed; employment type code | Preserve code; do not expand FT without dictionary. |
| Prism client.typeClass | F observed; employment type class | Preserve separately; no enumerated meanings supplied. |
| Prism ClientMaster.employeeType[] | typeCodeId only; empty saved catalog | No verified label table to publish. |
| isolved employmentCategoryCode | Current employment category code | Candidate employment_type_code; dictionary still needed. |
| UKG pay_info.employee_type.id | Numeric reference ID | Keep as string reference, not a descriptive employment type. |

UKG pay_info comes from the **separate employee pay-info endpoint**, represented by UKGReadyEmployeePayInfo; it is not a field invented inside employee detail. The existing supplemental container supplies it when available.

### Pay-rate basis and compensation

| Prism hourlyPayPeriod | Documented meaning | Proposed basis label |
|---|---|---|
| H | Hourly | hour |
| W | Weekly | week |
| B | Bi-weekly | bi-weekly |
| S | Semi-monthly | semi-monthly |
| M | Monthly | month |
| Y | Yearly | year |

These describe hourlyPayRate's basis despite its name. Proposed separate names include annual_salary, pay_period_salary, hourly_rate, other_pay_rate and other_pay_rate_basis. Explicit names preserve meaning; they do not authorize converting monthly/yearly rates or treating every amount as salary. Keep numeric conversion separate from basis conversion.

### Rehire

| Source | Value | Decided canonical value |
|---|---|---|
| Prism rehireOk | Y | eligible |
| Prism rehireOk | N | ineligible |
| Prism rehireOk | empty string | no preference |
| isolved eligibleForRehire | true / false | eligible / ineligible |
| Either | missing / null | null (unavailable, not no preference) |

### State and ZIP

| Source field | Evidence | Decided destination |
|---|---|---|
| Prism contactInformation.state | Description only says state | state_code candidate; retain raw and verify code-valued inputs. |
| isolved nameAddress.state | Explicitly a state code | state_code |
| UKG address.state_code | Live/source-model addition; no contract description here | state_code, with provenance retained. |
| UKG address.state | Live/source-model addition; meaning not described here | Preserve separately; state_name only when confirmed. |
| Prism zipcode + zipSuffix | ZIP + documented ZIP+4 extension | postal_code = ZIP-SUFFIX when suffix is nonblank. |
| isolved nameAddress.zipCode | Postal text | postal_code unchanged. |
| UKG address.zip | Live/source-model field | postal_code unchanged. |

No state abbreviation dictionary or ZIP-code catalog is supplied for these fields. No invented lookup table is needed for copying text. Do not add another suffix to an already extended ZIP; conflicting suffixes need review. Preserve leading zeros and original parts. A suffix alone must not become a complete postal code.

### Ethnicity

No shared ethnicity category is required **yet**. A shared category is useful only when consumers need comparable values across providers. Prism ethnicCode is organization-defined; isolved ethnicOrigin has no supplied category dictionary. Preserve both separately, plus Prism Hispanic Boolean. Do not invent race/ethnicity translations or merge those concepts.

### Company references and work location

| Source | Preserve as | Explanation |
|---|---|---|
| Prism person.employerIds[] | prismhr_employer_ids: list[str] | Employers associated with this person; preserve every element. No further decision needed to keep the list. |
| Prism CLI client ID | prismhr_client_id: str | Separate request/client context; not automatically an employer ID. |
| isolved legalCode | isolved_legal_code: str | Legal reference code; do not combine with Prism IDs. |
| UKG ein.id / ein_name | ukg_ein_id: str / ukg_ein_name: str | Preserve EIN record ID and name; no invented company ID. |
| Prism client.homeLocation | work_location_code candidate | User-confirmed employee work-location role; preserve the provider code. |
| isolved workLocation | work_location_code candidate | Explicit current work-location code; provider scopes remain distinct. |
| isolved workLocationDashDescription | isolved_work_location_display | Combined location ID-description text; keep whole value. |
| isolved organizations[] | isolved_organizations | Organizational assignments, not a work-location code. Preserve full list. |

The earlier source_company_association list/string wording proposed one shared name with inconsistent shapes. Replace it with the separate fields above for now. A common association list could be designed later, but is unnecessary for this step. The user confirms the employee home-location/work-location role for this task; code values remain provider-scoped.

School district codes remain provider-specific school/tax context; they have no established relationship to employment status.

### Evidence

- Prism OpenAPI: Person.gender/maritalStatus/ethnicCode/employerIds; Client.employeeStatus/statusClass/employeeType/typeClass/rehireOk/homeLocation; Compensation.hourlyPayPeriod; ContactInformation; EmployeeStatus and EmployeeType catalog schemas.
- Primary isolved Swagger: AOS.PayrollOffice.RestAPI.Models.EmployeeModel and EmployeeNameAddressModel. Corestream.EmployeeModel is explicitly a different schema.
- UKG cached employee and pay-info schemas, their HTML references, and existing typed source models. Supplemental pay-info is optional.
- All three local lookup-reference.json files: no complete field-specific employee status/type/marital/ethnicity dictionaries are supplied.
- Saved Postman responses: only the small non-identifying code/label examples above were retained; catalog arrays were empty. Samples are observations, not exhaustive dictionaries.

Business-label choices are decided. Missing dictionaries are handled by preservation without translation; there is no blocking user decision.

## Accepted readable labels — latest decisions

These decisions supersede earlier candidate labels and location caveats. Labels describe canonical meaning; undocumented source-code translations are not invented.

| Topic | Decided output |
|---|---|
| Gender | male, female, non-binary, declined, unknown; preserve blank separately from unknown. Reporting override/designation remain separate. |
| Marital status | single, married, divorced, widowed, civil union, other. Translate documented Prism codes and verified descriptive values; preserve unrecognized values separately. |
| Status class | active, on leave, terminated for documented Prism A/L/T. Configured employee status remains separate. |
| Employment type | full-time, part-time when source meanings are verified. No other provider employment-type label is verified in the supplied dictionaries; retain other raw values. Do not guess FT/F/PT/P meanings or interpret UKG numeric lookup IDs as labels. |
| Pay-rate basis | H=hourly, W=weekly, B=bi-weekly, S=semi-monthly, M=monthly, Y=yearly. No rate conversion implied. |
| Ethnicity | Preserve separately. Code-valued input alone does not establish a shared code dictionary. |
| Compensation | Explicit amount names plus documented basis; numeric conversion does not imply frequency/currency conversion. |
| Rehire | eligible, ineligible, no preference; missing/null stays unavailable. |
| Company references | Keep the already-decided separate provider references and list cardinality. |
| Work location | User confirms Prism homeLocation represents employee work location. Use the shared work_location_code concept; retain provider-specific code values and original display description/organizations. |

Work-location codes need not be globally standardized to preserve the shared concept: copy each provider's code with provider/company scope. A shared lookup is necessary only to translate codes into common locations or descriptions; none is fabricated.

No further business-label decision is needed from the user now. Missing dictionaries are evidence gaps to resolve during mapping, not repeated approval requests. Detailed helper behavior can be addressed when implementing those helpers.

## Implementation contract — start here

This is the first-mapper specification, not a request for more decisions. Unknown provider semantics stay preserved; they do not block implementation. All 1,170 inventoried source paths remain listed below; coverage refers to this documented snapshot, not proof of live API completeness.

### Output and boundaries

- Each employee mapper accepts an already-validated provider employee model, its validated supplemental records and supplied context. It does not repeat source model_validate calls or make API requests.
- Normalizers return a list of canonical employees, including [] for no employees. Build Python/Pydantic objects first. Serialize to JSON-compatible dictionaries only at the normalizer output boundary; the CLI performs final JSON encoding. Optional output dates serialize YYYY-MM-DD.
- Canonical root fields: provider: str; employee_id: Optional[str] (key required, null retained where accepted by the source); every flat target and type in the inventory; provider_fields: dict[str, Any]; source_metadata: dict[str, Any]; source_extras: dict[str, Any]. Other mapped business fields are optional. No manufactured identity or defaults.
- provider_fields contains provider-keyed, nested known fields for dotted targets such as prismhr.person.*. Flat named provider fields such as prismhr_employer_ids and ukg_ein_id keep their explicit names. Use nested dictionaries/typed submodels rather than literal dotted JSON keys.
- source_metadata contains provider wrapper/messages/paging/links, original_values for transformed or unresolved values, and mapping_issues (each with source path and reason). Do not silently turn catalog/list metadata into employee business fields. Repeated per-employee wrapper metadata is acceptable in this first version.
- source_extras is provider-keyed, preserving original nesting and list positions for undeclared model_extra fields. Recursively extract extras; they cannot overwrite declared canonical fields. Unknown fields are not claimed to have source-model validation.
- Preserve absent/null/empty/zero/false distinctions through source model_fields_set and raw/original-value metadata. A missing optional collection remains missing, null remains null and an explicitly supplied empty list remains []; the outer no-employee result remains [].

### Helpers and fallback rules

| Helper/topic | First implementation rule |
|---|---|
| Identifier | Keep strings unchanged, including leading zeros. Convert integers and finite integral floats without a trailing .0. Reject fractional/nonfinite numeric identifiers with a path-specific ValueError; never truncate. Preserve accepted null IDs. |
| Date | Missing/null -> None; blank -> None with original value retained. Parse ISO date or supported ISO datetime and keep its written calendar date without timezone shifting. Retain original timestamp/offset. Invalid values -> None plus original value and mapping issue; never invent a date. No fuzzy date guessing. |
| Numeric amount/hours | Parse nonblank numeric strings into finite float. Keep zero and negative values. Blank/invalid/nonfinite -> None plus original value and issue. Do not strip currency symbols, round, rescale percentages or infer units. |
| Readable labels | Translate only documented codes, user-approved assumptions, or verified descriptive labels. Match known descriptive labels case-insensitively. Preserve unknown raw values in original_values/provider_fields and record an issue; leave the shared value None. Blank stays blank for label fields unless an explicit rule says otherwise. |
| Gender | M/F/X/D -> male/female/non-binary/declined as user-approved Prism assumptions; U -> unknown as user extension. B has no mapping. Ordinary gender, EEO override and Prism designation retain separate fields; never apply ordinary-gender mappings to the other domains automatically. |
| Marital | Prism S/M/D/W/C/O -> single/married/divorced/widowed/civil union/other. isolved descriptive Married -> married; other verified complete labels use the same vocabulary. Unverified single-character isolved codes are not decoded using the Prism dictionary. |
| Status/type | Prism statusClass A/L/T -> active/on leave/terminated. Configured status and type codes use only supplied field-specific dictionaries; no dictionary -> preserve raw and leave descriptive target None. Full-time/part-time are approved labels, not proof of FT/F/PT/P source meanings. UKG type ID stays ukg_employee_type_id. |
| Rate basis | H/W/B/S/M/Y -> hourly/weekly/bi-weekly/semi-monthly/monthly/yearly. No rate conversion. |
| Rehire | Prism Y/N/blank -> eligible/ineligible/no preference. isolved true/false -> eligible/ineligible. Missing/null -> None, not no preference. Unknown code -> None plus original value and issue. |
| ZIP | ZIP plus nonblank suffix -> ZIP-SUFFIX. Do not duplicate an identical suffix already present. Missing base or a conflicting suffix -> retain base as supplied, preserve components and record an issue. Never coerce ZIP text to numbers. |
| State/location | Copy code strings with provider scope. Preserve UKG address.state as ukg_state_value until its meaning is verified. User accepts Prism homeLocation and isolved workLocation as the shared work-location-code concept; no cross-provider code equivalence or description lookup is implied. |

### Conflicts, collections and mapper structure

- Use one explicit map_<provider>_employee function and small helpers for nested sections/lists. Map nested objects only when supplied; map each list item independently in order. Do not assign a whole mapped parent and then overwrite it with a second competing parent rule.
- The inventory lists containers and their children for coverage; implement one recursive mapping traversal, not two independent copies of the same data. Each source path has one primary destination; an original-value copy is provenance, not a second competing mapping rule.
- Preferred pronouns: copy the sole supplied value, or one exact value when both agree. If two nonblank values disagree, leave preferred_pronouns None, preserve both with their source paths and record a conflict. No arbitrary priority.
- Distinct hire/rehire/updated-hire and job-start/change/effective dates now have separate inventory destinations. Do not choose a maximum date or an undocumented fallback.
- Detail records are primary business records. Employee-list wrappers remain source_metadata; do not automatically join list records to detail records. Native list input already accepted by validation maps one employee per validated list record; do not treat a sparse list item as complete detail.
- Supplemental salary history, pay-info and compensation history retain their named boundaries. Attach only when the existing validated structure or explicit supplied identity/context establishes the owner. Never attach an unowned supplemental record to every employee or infer ownership from list position; preserve it separately with an issue if ownership is unresolved.
- No sorting, deduplication, source-to-source API joins, sum/reconciliation, inferred employee/company identity or generated global IDs. Prism CLI client_id remains separate from person.employerIds.
- reference can supply explicitly scoped documented lookup dictionaries; it is not source input context by default. Missing dictionaries use preservation fallback. Do not substitute company, user or payroll status dictionaries for employee status.

### Implementation acceptance checks

1. Every inventory source path is consumed by a shared mapping, preserved named/provider field, metadata or recursive extras handling.
2. The same output target has one canonical type. Repeated-target rules are limited to explicit aggregation (postal code) and conflict handling (preferred pronouns), not last-write-wins assignment.
3. Verify representative nested records; null/absent/empty; zero/false; nullable IDs; integral/fractional identifiers; invalid/offset dates; invalid amounts; unknown/blank codes; ZIP suffix conflicts; pronoun conflicts; multiple employees and supplemental ownership.
4. Check that raw unresolved values and nested extras survive output serialization, and that mapping performs no API calls or duplicate source validation.
5. Implement the common canonical classes and helpers, then Prism mapper first, followed by isolved and UKG using the same output contract. Existing validation-only stubs still need their mapping return path implemented; this document does not claim they already return normalized employees.


## isolved employment-category preservation — latest decision

- Preserve employmentCategoryCode unchanged at provider_fields.isolved.employment_category_code; descriptive employment_type remains unset. No empty label lookup or guessed meanings.
- Links remain in provider_fields.isolved.unmapped with nesting/list positions intact.
- Standalone salary-history input has no employee owner: preserve its records together with employee_id=null and an explicit ownership issue; salary-record IDs never become employee IDs.
